- **design-review**: 当需要评审方案设计、检查设计漏洞、进行方案把关时自动使用此 Skill。充当方案评审者角色，逐维度审查方案的完整性、可行性和风险。 触发场景： - 方案/设计文档完成后需要评审把关 - 头脑风暴结束后需要验证方案是否完备 - 实施计划制定后需要检查是否有遗漏 - 技术方案需要多维度审查（安全/性能/多租户/可维护性） - 架构设计完成后需要对抗性审查 - 需求文档需要检查边界条件和异常路径 触发词：方案评审、设计评审、审查方案、检查方案、评审设计、把关、设计审查、design review、检查漏洞、过一遍方案、方案有问题吗、有没有遗漏、方案检查、架构评审、需求评审 ([linxibo2022-web/ruoyi](https://github.com/linxibo2022-web/ruoyi/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/design-review) · 2 copies)

([muhammaddadu/ai-skill-collection](https://github.com/muhammaddadu/ai-skill-collection/tree/HEAD/2-discovery/design-signal) · ★1)
- **design-signal-chain**: Use when routing audio through processing plugins or hardware and need to determine the optimal processing order ([jeffreytse/grimoire-core](https://github.com/jeffreytse/grimoire-core/tree/HEAD/skills/music/production/skills/design-signal-chain) · ★8)
- **design-signal-change-and-clearance-intervals**: Use this skill when the user wants to design or analyze signal CHANGE AND CLEARANCE INTERVALS (yellow and all-red timing) in SUMO as a first-class safety-and-capacity design object, rather than accepting a hardcoded default — testing for the classical dilemma zone, the safety-capacity tradeoff of interval length, whether measured lost time matches the assumed intergreen, and how heavy vehicles/grade/intersection geometry shift the required interval. Covers an ITE/kinematic analytic reference calculator (required yellow and all-red formulas, stopping-distance vs clearing-distance dilemma-zone… ([qiliuchn/SimSkill-V1](https://github.com/qiliuchn/SimSkill-V1/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/procedural-memory/design-signal-change-and-clearance-intervals) · ★1)
